This is completely unprecedented for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that people were trying to harm you, spy on you, or that you were being targeted by others in any other situations outside of this specific work data issue?\n\nPatient: No, I've never felt spied on, targeted, or harmed in any other part of my life. My concern is entirely about the water-use data at work being secretly manipulated, and it's deeply frustrating that everyone just dismisses the clear proof I've found.\n\nDoctor: Besides the data manipulation at work, have you ever believed that you have special abilities, a secret mission, or that common events are specifically designed to send you a personal message?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, and I'm a bit confused why you'd ask that—I don't have any \"special powers\" or secret missions. I'm not crazy; my concern is strictly about the concrete, logical data manipulation I have mathematically proven in the spreadsheets at work. It's a real, objective issue with the numbers, not some kind of fantasy.\n\nDoctor: Since this is the first time you have experienced these symptoms, have you felt this way continuously for at least two years, or has there been any period during these last two years where you felt like your normal, energetic self?"
  }

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
No, I haven't had any breaks or times where I felt like my old self; it has been a continuous, steady slide for a little over two years now. Ever since that big consulting contract and software migration started, I've just gotten progressively more withdrawn and consumed by these data issues.

My firm has even suggested I take a leave of absence, which is why my wife persuaded me to come see you.\n\nDoctor: Besides these concerns about your work, have you experienced any periods where your mood was significantly elevated, depressed, or where your energy levels were unusually high or low?\n\nPatient: Lately, my energy has been incredibly low, and I mostly just sit around staring at the spreadsheets because I don't have the interest or drive to do anything else. I definitely haven't had any periods where my mood was unusually high or elevated. It's just been a complete lack of energy and feeling flat.\n\nDoctor: Beyond these concerns about your work, have you noticed any changes in your ability to organize your thoughts, or have you experienced any unusual sensory experiences, such as hearing voices or seeing things that others do not?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't heard any voices or seen anything unusual, and my thoughts are perfectly organized. The only thing I'm focused on is the very real evidence of data manipulation in the spreadsheets at work. It's not a trick of my mind; someone is actively tampering with those files.\n\nDoctor: Are your feelings of low energy and lack of interest present even when you are not thinking about the situation at work, or do they only occur when you are focused on those concerns?\n\nPatient: It's present all the time and has taken over my whole life, not just when I'm looking at the spreadsheets. I've completely stopped going to my hiking and star-gazing clubs, and I barely even talk to my wife because I feel completely flat and disengaged from everything. I just don't have the energy or interest for anything else anymore.\n\nDoctor: Over the past two weeks, have you been experiencing physical symptoms like significant changes in your sleep, appetite, or feeling physically slowed down to the point where others might notice?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed any significant changes in my sleep or appetite. While I do spend a lot of time just sitting around with very little energy or interest, I'm not sure if I'm physically slowed down in the way you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that your conviction about the data manipulation is the primary cause of your low mood, or did you feel this sense of being flat and disengaged even before you became concerned about the situation at work?\n\nPatient: The flat, disengaged feeling actually started much earlier, sliding gradually over the last two years as I stopped hiking and star-gazing. My certainty about the data being tampered with is much more recent, only developing over the last couple of months. So the lack of energy and withdrawal definitely came first.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced similar episodes of feeling this flat, low-energy, and disengaged in the past, or is this the first time you have felt this way for such a long period?\n\nPatient: No, I have never felt like this before, and this is the first time in my life I've experienced this kind of flat state. Before this slide started about two and a half years ago with a high-stakes work contract and software migration, I was very active and always had energy for hiking and star-gazing. This prolonged feeling of being completely disconnected and drained is entirely new to me.\n\nDoctor: Has there been any other time in your life, perhaps years ago, where you experienced a distinct period of a few weeks or more where you felt similarly low, flat, or lacked interest in your hobbies?\n\nPatient: No, I don't recall ever feeling this way or losing interest in my hobbies at any other time in my life prior to this. My concern is entirely about the water-use data at work being secretly manipulated, and it's deeply frustrating that everyone just dismisses the clear proof I've found.\n\nDoctor: Besides the data manipulation at work, have you ever believed that you have special abilities, a secret mission, or that common events are specifically designed to send you a personal message?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, and I'm a bit confused why you'd ask that—I don't have any \"special powers\" or secret missions. I'm not crazy; my concern is strictly about the concrete, logical data manipulation I have mathematically proven in the spreadsheets at work. It's a real, objective issue with the numbers, not some kind of fantasy.\n\nDoctor: Since this is the first time you have experienced these symptoms, have you felt this way continuously for at least two years, or has there been any period during these last two years where you felt like your normal, energetic self?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had any breaks or times where I felt like my old self; it has been a continuous, steady slide for a little over two years now. Ever since that big consulting contract and software migration started, I've just gotten progressively more withdrawn and consumed by these data issues.\n\nDoctor: During these past two years, have you experienced any other symptoms like difficulty concentrating, feelings of hopelessness, or low self-esteem in addition to your low energy and loss of interest?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced hopelessness, low self-esteem, or any trouble concentrating. I just don't have the energy or interest for anything else anymore.\n\nDoctor: Over the past two weeks, have you been experiencing physical symptoms like significant changes in your sleep, appetite, or feeling physically slowed down to the point where others might notice?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed any significant changes in my sleep or appetite. While I do spend a lot of time just sitting around with very little energy or interest, I'm not sure if I'm physically slowed down in the way you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that your conviction about the data manipulation is the primary cause of your low mood, or did you feel this sense of being flat and disengaged even before you became concerned about the situation at work?\n\nPatient: The flat, disengaged feeling actually started much earlier, sliding gradually over the last two years as I stopped hiking and star-gazing. My certainty about the data being tampered with is much more recent, only developing over the last couple of months. So the lack of energy and withdrawal definitely came first.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced similar episodes of feeling this flat, low-energy, and disengaged in the past, or is this the first time you have felt this way for such a long period?\n\nPatient: No, I have never felt like this before, and this is the first time in my life I've experienced this kind of flat state. Before this slide started about two and a half years ago with a high-stakes work contract and software migration, I was very active and always had energy for hiking and star-gazing. This prolonged feeling of being completely disconnected and drained is entirely new to me.\n\nDoctor: Has there been any other time in your life, perhaps years ago, where you experienced a distinct period of a few weeks or more where you felt similarly low, flat, or lacked interest in your hobbies?\n\nPatient: No, I don't recall ever feeling this way or losing interest in my hobbies at any other time in my life prior to this. Before this situation started a couple of years ago, I was always consistently active and engaged with my interests. This is completely unprecedented for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that people were trying to harm you, spy on you, or that you were being targeted by others in any other situations outside of this specific work data issue?\n\nPatient: No, I've never felt spied on, targeted, or harmed in any other part of my life. My concern is entirely about the water-use data at work being secretly manipulated, and it's deeply frustrating that everyone just dismisses the clear proof I've found.\n\nDoctor: Besides the data manipulation at work, have you ever believed that you have special abilities, a secret mission, or that common events are specifically designed to send you a personal message?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, and I'm a bit confused why you'd ask that—I don't have any \"special powers\" or secret missions. I'm not crazy; my concern is strictly about the concrete, logical data manipulation I have mathematically proven in the spreadsheets at work. It's a real, objective issue with the numbers, not some kind of fantasy.\n\nDoctor: Since this is the first time you have experienced these symptoms, have you felt this way continuously for at least two years, or has there been any period during these last two years where you felt like your normal, energetic self?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had any breaks or times where I felt like my old self; it has been a continuous, steady slide for a little over two years now. Ever since that big consulting contract and software migration started, I've just gotten progressively more withdrawn and consumed by these data issues.\n\nDoctor: During these past two years, have you experienced any other symptoms like difficulty concentrating, feelings of hopelessness, or low self-esteem in addition to your low energy and loss of interest?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced hopelessness, low self-esteem, or any trouble concentrating. My focus is actually intensely locked onto those data patterns, and I don't feel bad about myself; I am just extremely drained and have lost interest in everything else.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite, sleep patterns, or feelings of guilt or worthlessness during these past two years, or has it strictly been a loss of energy and interest?\n\nPatient: It has strictly been a loss of energy, interest, and emotion, as I don't feel guilty or worthless at all. My sleep is sometimes delayed because I stay up late analyzing the spreadsheets, but my appetite is normal and I don't feel bad about myself. I simply have no drive or energy to do or feel anything outside of this work issue."

I just don't have the energy or interest for anything else anymore.\n\nDoctor: Over the past two weeks, have you been experiencing physical symptoms like significant changes in your sleep, appetite, or feeling physically slowed down to the point where others might notice?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed any significant changes in my sleep or appetite. While I do spend a lot of time just sitting around with very little energy or interest, I'm not sure if I'm physically slowed down in the way you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that your conviction about the data manipulation is the primary cause of your low mood, or did you feel this sense of being flat and disengaged even before you became concerned about the situation at work?\n\nPatient: The flat, disengaged feeling actually started much earlier, sliding gradually over the last two years as I stopped hiking and star-gazing. My certainty about the data being tampered with is much more recent, only developing over the last couple of months. So the lack of energy and withdrawal definitely came first.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced similar episodes of feeling this flat, low-energy, and disengaged in the past, or is this the first time you have felt this way for such a long period?\n\nPatient: No, I have never felt like this before, and this is the first time in my life I've experienced this kind of flat state. Before this slide started about two and a half years ago with a high-stakes work contract and software migration, I was very active and always had energy for hiking and star-gazing. This prolonged feeling of being completely disconnected and drained is entirely new to me.\n\nDoctor: Has there been any other time in your life, perhaps years ago, where you experienced a distinct period of a few weeks or more where you felt similarly low, flat, or lacked interest in your hobbies?\n\nPatient: No, I don't recall ever feeling this way or losing interest in my hobbies at any other time in my life prior to this. Before this situation started a couple of years ago, I was always consistently active and engaged with my interests. This is completely unprecedented for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that people were trying to harm you, spy on you, or that you were being targeted by others in any other situations outside of this specific work data issue?\n\nPatient: No, I've never felt spied on, targeted, or harmed in any other part of my life. My concern is entirely about the water-use data at work being secretly manipulated, and it's deeply frustrating that everyone just dismisses the clear proof I've found.\n\nDoctor: Besides the data manipulation at work, have you ever believed that you have special abilities, a secret mission, or that common events are specifically designed to send you a personal message?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, and I'm a bit confused why you'd ask that—I don't have any \"special powers\" or secret missions. I'm not crazy; my concern is strictly about the concrete, logical data manipulation I have mathematically proven in the spreadsheets at work. It's a real, objective issue with the numbers, not some kind of fantasy.\n\nDoctor: Since this is the first time you have experienced these symptoms, have you felt this way continuously for at least two years, or has there been any period during these last two years where you felt like your normal, energetic self?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had any breaks or times where I felt like my old self; it has been a continuous, steady slide for a little over two years now. Ever since that big consulting contract and software migration started, I've just gotten progressively more withdrawn and consumed by these data issues.\n\nDoctor: During these past two years, have you experienced any other symptoms like difficulty concentrating, feelings of hopelessness, or low self-esteem in addition to your low energy and loss of interest?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced hopelessness, low self-esteem, or any trouble concentrating. My focus is actually intensely locked onto those data patterns, and I don't feel bad about myself; I am just extremely drained and have lost interest in everything else.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite, sleep patterns, or feelings of guilt or worthlessness during these past two years, or has it strictly been a loss of energy and interest?\n\nPatient: It has strictly been a loss of energy, interest, and emotion, as I don't feel guilty or worthless at all. My sleep is sometimes delayed because I stay up late analyzing the spreadsheets, but my appetite is normal and I don't feel bad about myself. I simply have no drive or energy to do or feel anything outside of this work issue.\n\nDoctor: Regarding the data manipulation you've discovered, do you believe that you are the only one who has the intelligence or special insight to uncover this, or do you feel you are being targeted by these people because of who you are?\n\nPatient: I'm not sure about being targeted or having unique intelligence, but as a professional, the mathematical patterns in the data are simply clear to me. It's just incredibly frustrating that everyone else is dismissing and ignoring these obvious signs."
  }

It's just been a complete lack of energy and feeling flat.\n\nDoctor: Beyond these concerns about your work, have you noticed any changes in your ability to organize your thoughts, or have you experienced any unusual sensory experiences, such as hearing voices or seeing things that others do not?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't heard any voices or seen anything unusual, and my thoughts are perfectly organized. The only thing I'm focused on is the very real evidence of data manipulation in the spreadsheets at work. It's not a trick of my mind; someone is actively tampering with those files.\n\nDoctor: Are your feelings of low energy and lack of interest present even when you are not thinking about the situation at work, or do they only occur when you are focused on those concerns?\n\nPatient: It's present all the time and has taken over my whole life, not just when I'm looking at the spreadsheets. I've completely stopped going to my hiking and star-gazing clubs, and I barely even talk to my wife because I feel completely flat and disengaged from everything. I just don't have the energy or interest for anything else anymore.\n\nDoctor: Over the past two weeks, have you been experiencing physical symptoms like significant changes in your sleep, appetite, or feeling physically slowed down to the point where others might notice?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed any significant changes in my sleep or appetite. While I do spend a lot of time just sitting around with very little energy or interest, I'm not sure if I'm physically slowed down in the way you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that your conviction about the data manipulation is the primary cause of your low mood, or did you feel this sense of being flat and disengaged even before you became concerned about the situation at work?\n\nPatient: The flat, disengaged feeling actually started much earlier, sliding gradually over the last two years as I stopped hiking and star-gazing. My certainty about the data being tampered with is much more recent, only developing over the last couple of months. So the lack of energy and withdrawal definitely came first.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced similar episodes of feeling this flat, low-energy, and disengaged in the past, or is this the first time you have felt this way for such a long period?\n\nPatient: No, I have never felt like this before, and this is the first time in my life I've experienced this kind of flat state. Before this slide started about two and a half years ago with a high-stakes work contract and software migration, I was very active and always had energy for hiking and star-gazing. This prolonged feeling of being completely disconnected and drained is entirely new to me.\n\nDoctor: Has there been any other time in your life, perhaps years ago, where you experienced a distinct period of a few weeks or more where you felt similarly low, flat, or lacked interest in your hobbies?\n\nPatient: No, I don't recall ever feeling this way or losing interest in my hobbies at any other time in my life prior to this. Before this situation started a couple of years ago, I was always consistently active and engaged with my interests. This is completely unprecedented for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that people were trying to harm you, spy on you, or that you were being targeted by others in any other situations outside of this specific work data issue?\n\nPatient: No, I've never felt spied on, targeted, or harmed in any other part of my life. My concern is entirely about the water-use data at work being secretly manipulated, and it's deeply frustrating that everyone just dismisses the clear proof I've found.\n\nDoctor: Besides the data manipulation at work, have you ever believed that you have special abilities, a secret mission, or that common events are specifically designed to send you a personal message?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, and I'm a bit confused why you'd ask that—I don't have any \"special powers\" or secret missions. I'm not crazy; my concern is strictly about the concrete, logical data manipulation I have mathematically proven in the spreadsheets at work. It's a real, objective issue with the numbers, not some kind of fantasy.\n\nDoctor: Since this is the first time you have experienced these symptoms, have you felt this way continuously for at least two years, or has there been any period during these last two years where you felt like your normal, energetic self?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had any breaks or times where I felt like my old self; it has been a continuous, steady slide for a little over two years now. Ever since that big consulting contract and software migration started, I've just gotten progressively more withdrawn and consumed by these data issues.\n\nDoctor: During these past two years, have you experienced any other symptoms like difficulty concentrating, feelings of hopelessness, or low self-esteem in addition to your low energy and loss of interest?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced hopelessness, low self-esteem, or any trouble concentrating. My focus is actually intensely locked onto those data patterns, and I don't feel bad about myself; I am just extremely drained and have lost interest in everything else.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite, sleep patterns, or feelings of guilt or worthlessness during these past two years, or has it strictly been a loss of energy and interest?\n\nPatient: It has strictly been a loss of energy, interest, and emotion, as I don't feel guilty or worthless at all. My sleep is sometimes delayed because I stay up late analyzing the spreadsheets, but my appetite is normal and I don't feel bad about myself. I simply have no drive or energy to do or feel anything outside of this work issue.\n\nDoctor: Regarding the data manipulation you've discovered, do you believe that you are the only one who has the intelligence or special insight to uncover this, or do you feel you are being targeted by these people because of who you are?\n\nPatient: I'm not sure about being targeted or having unique intelligence, but as a professional, the mathematical patterns in the data are simply clear to me. It's just incredibly frustrating that everyone else is dismissing and ignoring these obvious signs.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other symptoms like feeling indecisive or having a generally pessimistic view of your future during these past two years?\n\nPatient: I don't feel indecisive at all; if anything, I am completely certain about the data manipulations I've uncovered. I also haven't really thought about a pessimistic view of the future. My mind is entirely occupied right now with trying to solve this data problem at work."
  }
]

I believe someone inside the agency or higher up in government is quietly altering the data and slipping in bogus parameters, though I don't know if they are targeting me personally. However, whenever I point out the patterns, everyone just smiles, says it's "within tolerance," and treats me like I'm imagining things. It really feels like they are just trying to cover it up and ignore my warnings.

I simply have no drive or energy to do or feel anything outside of this work issue.\n\nDoctor: Regarding the data manipulation you've discovered, do you believe that you are the only one who has the intelligence or special insight to uncover this, or do you feel you are being targeted by these people because of who you are?\n\nPatient: I'm not sure about being targeted or having unique intelligence, but as a professional, the mathematical patterns in the data are simply clear to me. It's just incredibly frustrating that everyone else is dismissing and ignoring these obvious signs.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other symptoms like feeling indecisive or having a generally pessimistic view of your future during these past two years?\n\nPatient: I don't feel indecisive at all; if anything, I am completely certain about the data manipulations I've uncovered. My concern is entirely about the water-use data at work being secretly manipulated, and it's deeply frustrating that everyone just dismisses the clear proof I've found.\n\nDoctor: Besides the data manipulation at work, have you ever believed that you have special abilities, a secret mission, or that common events are specifically designed to send you a personal message?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, and I'm a bit confused why you'd ask that—I don't have any \"special powers\" or secret missions. I'm not crazy; my concern is strictly about the concrete, logical data manipulation I have mathematically proven in the spreadsheets at work. It's a real, objective issue with the numbers, not some kind of fantasy.\n\nDoctor: Since this is the first time you have experienced these symptoms, have you felt this way continuously for at least two years, or has there been any period during these last two years where you felt like your normal, energetic self?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had any breaks or times where I felt like my old self; it has been a continuous, steady slide for a little over two years now. Ever since that big consulting contract and software migration started, I've just gotten progressively more withdrawn and consumed by these data issues.\n\nDoctor: During these past two years, have you experienced any other symptoms like difficulty concentrating, feelings of hopelessness, or low self-esteem in addition to your low energy and loss of interest?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced hopelessness, low self-esteem, or any trouble concentrating. My focus is actually intensely locked onto those data patterns, and I don't feel bad about myself; I am just extremely drained and have lost interest in everything else.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite, sleep patterns, or feelings of guilt or worthlessness during these past two years, or has it strictly been a loss of energy and interest?\n\nPatient: It has strictly been a loss of energy, interest, and emotion, as I don't feel guilty or worthless at all. My sleep is sometimes delayed because I stay up late analyzing the spreadsheets, but my appetite is normal and I don't feel bad about myself. I simply have no drive or energy to do or feel anything outside of this work issue.\n\nDoctor: Regarding the data manipulation you've discovered, do you believe that you are the only one who has the intelligence or special insight to uncover this, or do you feel you are being targeted by these people because of who you are?\n\nPatient: I'm not sure about being targeted or having unique intelligence, but as a professional, the mathematical patterns in the data are simply clear to me. It's just incredibly frustrating that everyone else is dismissing and ignoring these obvious signs.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other symptoms like feeling indecisive or having a generally pessimistic view of your future during these past two years?\n\nPatient: I don't feel indecisive at all; if anything, I am completely certain about the data manipulations I've uncovered. I also haven't really thought about a pessimistic view of the future. My mind is entirely occupied right now with trying to solve this data problem at work.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these data manipulations are part of a larger, organized plot by your colleagues or the company to specifically obstruct or silence you?\n\nPatient: I believe someone inside the agency or higher up in government is quietly altering the data and slipping in bogus parameters, though I don't know if they are targeting me personally. However, whenever I point out the patterns, everyone just smiles, says it's \"within tolerance,\" and treats me like I'm imagining things. It really feels like they are just trying to cover it up and ignore my warnings.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these individuals are intentionally conspiring or acting together to cover up these data manipulations because you are the one who discovered them?"
  }
]
========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Yes, it absolutely feels like a coordinated effort to cover it up and discredit me because I'm the one who noticed their fingerprints on the data. Whenever I point out the clear patterns of manipulation in the spreadsheets and emails, they just smile, dismiss it as being "within tolerance," and treat me like I'm imagining things. It is deeply frustrating that they are trying to make me look crazy just because I'm the one who uncovered what they did.

I definitely haven't had any periods where my mood was unusually high or elevated. It's just been a complete lack of energy and feeling flat.\n\nDoctor: Beyond these concerns about your work, have you noticed any changes in your ability to organize your thoughts, or have you experienced any unusual sensory experiences, such as hearing voices or seeing things that others do not?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't heard any voices or seen anything unusual, and my thoughts are perfectly organized. The only thing I'm focused on is the very real evidence of data manipulation in the spreadsheets at work. It's not a trick of my mind; someone is actively tampering with those files.\n\nDoctor: Are your feelings of low energy and lack of interest present even when you are not thinking about the situation at work, or do they only occur when you are focused on those concerns?\n\nPatient: It's present all the time and has taken over my whole life, not just when I'm looking at the spreadsheets. I've completely stopped going to my hiking and star-gazing clubs, and I barely even talk to my wife because I feel completely flat and disengaged from everything. While I do spend a lot of time just sitting around with very little energy or interest, I'm not sure if I'm physically slowed down in the way you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that your conviction about the data manipulation is the primary cause of your low mood, or did you feel this sense of being flat and disengaged even before you became concerned about the situation at work?\n\nPatient: The flat, disengaged feeling actually started much earlier, sliding gradually over the last two years as I stopped hiking and star-gazing. My certainty about the data being tampered with is much more recent, only developing over the last couple of months. My concern is entirely about the water-use data at work being secretly manipulated, and it's deeply frustrating that everyone just dismisses the clear proof I've found.\n\nDoctor: Besides the data manipulation at work, have you ever believed that you have special abilities, a secret mission, or that common events are specifically designed to send you a personal message?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, and I'm a bit confused why you'd ask that—I don't have any \"special powers\" or secret missions. I simply have no drive or energy to do or feel anything outside of this work issue.\n\nDoctor: Regarding the data manipulation you've discovered, do you believe that you are the only one who has the intelligence or special insight to uncover this, or do you feel you are being targeted by these people because of who you are?\n\nPatient: I'm not sure about being targeted or having unique intelligence, but as a professional, the mathematical patterns in the data are simply clear to me. Reply naturally in English as the Patient to the last Doctor utterance.\nAnswer only the question, in 2–3 sentences maximum.\n\n[Dialogue]\nDoctor: Hello, I am Dr. Smith. Could you please tell me what brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: I’m here because I’m absolutely certain there are serious problems and hidden manipulations in the data at work, but no one believes me and it’s starting to cost me my job. My firm has even suggested I take a leave of absence, which is why my wife persuaded me to come see you.\n\nDoctor: Besides these concerns about your work, have you experienced any periods where your mood was significantly elevated, depressed, or where your energy levels were unusually high or low?\n\nPatient: Lately, my energy has been incredibly low, and I mostly just sit around staring at the spreadsheets because I don't have the interest or drive to do anything else. I definitely haven't had any periods where my mood was unusually high or elevated. It's just been a complete lack of energy and feeling flat.\n\nDoctor: Beyond these concerns about your work, have you noticed any changes in your ability to organize your thoughts, or have you experienced any unusual sensory experiences, such as hearing voices or seeing things that others do not?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't heard any voices or seen anything unusual, and my thoughts are perfectly organized. The only thing I'm focused on is the very real evidence of data manipulation in the spreadsheets at work. It's not a trick of my mind; someone is actively tampering with those files.\n\nDoctor: Are your feelings of low energy and lack of interest present even when you are not thinking about the situation at work, or do they only occur when you are focused on those concerns?\n\nPatient: It's present all the time and has taken over my whole life, not just when I'm looking at the spreadsheets. I've completely stopped going to my hiking and star-gazing clubs, and I barely even talk to my wife because I feel completely flat and disengaged from everything. I just don't have the energy or interest for anything else anymore.\n\nDoctor: Over the past two weeks, have you been experiencing physical symptoms like significant changes in your sleep, appetite, or feeling physically slowed down to the point where others might notice?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed any significant changes in my sleep or appetite. While I do spend a lot of time just sitting around with very little energy or interest, I'm not sure if I'm physically slowed down in the way you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that your conviction about the data manipulation is the primary cause of your low mood, or did you feel this sense of being flat and disengaged even before you became concerned about the situation at work?\n\nPatient: The flat, disengaged feeling actually started much earlier, sliding gradually over the last two years as I stopped hiking and star-gazing. My certainty about the data being tampered with is much more recent, only developing over the last couple of months. So the lack of energy and withdrawal definitely came first.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced similar episodes of feeling this flat, low-energy, and disengaged in the past, or is this the first time you have felt this way for such a long period?\n\nPatient: No, I have never felt like this before, and this is the first time in my life I've experienced this kind of flat state. Before this slide started about two and a half years ago with a high-stakes work contract and software migration, I was very active and always had energy for hiking and star-gazing. This prolonged feeling of being completely disconnected and drained is entirely new to me.\n\nDoctor: Has there been any other time in your life, perhaps years ago, where you experienced a distinct period of a few weeks or more where you felt similarly low, flat, or lacked interest in your hobbies?\n\nPatient: No, I don't recall ever feeling this way or losing interest in my hobbies at any other time in my life prior to this. Before this situation started a couple of years ago, I was always consistently active and engaged with my interests. This is completely unprecedented for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that people were trying to harm you, spy on you, or that you were being targeted by others in any other situations outside of this specific work data issue?\n\nPatient: No, I've never felt spied on, targeted, or harmed in any other part of my life. My concern is entirely about the water-use data at work being secretly manipulated, and it's deeply frustrating that everyone just dismisses the clear proof I've found.\n\nDoctor: Besides the data manipulation at work, have you ever believed that you have special abilities, a secret mission, or that common events are specifically designed to send you a personal message?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, and I'm a bit confused why you'd ask that—I don't have any \"special powers\" or secret missions. I'm not crazy; my concern is strictly about the concrete, logical data manipulation I have mathematically proven in the spreadsheets at work. It's a real, objective issue with the numbers, not some kind of fantasy.\n\nDoctor: Since this is the first time you have experienced these symptoms, have you felt this way continuously for at least two years, or has there been any period during these last two years where you felt like your normal, energetic self?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had any breaks or times where I felt like my old self; it has been a continuous, steady slide for a little over two years now. Ever since that big consulting contract and software migration started, I've just gotten progressively more withdrawn and consumed by these data issues.\n\nDoctor: During these past two years, have you experienced any other symptoms like difficulty concentrating, feelings of hopelessness, or low self-esteem in addition to your low energy and loss of interest?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't experienced hopelessness, low self-esteem, or any trouble concentrating. My focus is actually intensely locked onto those data patterns, and I don't feel bad about myself; I am just extremely drained and have lost interest in everything else.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite, sleep patterns, or feelings of guilt or worthlessness during these past two years, or has it strictly been a loss of energy and interest?\n\nPatient: It has strictly been a loss of energy, interest, and emotion, as I don't feel guilty or worthless at all. My sleep is sometimes delayed because I stay up late analyzing the spreadsheets, but my appetite is normal and I don't feel bad about myself. I simply have no drive or energy to do or feel anything outside of this work issue.\n\nDoctor: Regarding the data manipulation you've discovered, do you believe that you are the only one who has the intelligence or special insight to uncover this, or do you feel you are being targeted by these people because of who you are?\n\nPatient: I'm not sure about being targeted or having unique intelligence, but as a professional, the mathematical patterns in the data are simply clear to me. It's just incredibly frustrating that everyone else is dismissing and ignoring these obvious signs.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other symptoms like feeling indecisive or having a generally pessimistic view of your future during these past two years?\n\nPatient: I don't feel indecisive at all; if anything, I am completely certain about the data manipulations I've uncovered. I also haven't really thought about a pessimistic view of the future. My mind is entirely occupied right now with trying to solve this data problem at work.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these data manipulations are part of a larger, organized plot by your colleagues or the company to specifically obstruct or silence you?\n\nPatient: I believe someone inside the agency or higher up in government is quietly altering the data and slipping in bogus parameters, though I don't know if they are targeting me personally. However, whenever I point out the patterns, everyone just smiles, says it's \"within tolerance,\" and treats me like I'm imagining things. It really feels like they are just trying to cover it up and ignore my warnings.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these individuals are intentionally conspiring or acting together to cover up these data manipulations because you are the one who discovered them?\n\nPatient: Yes, it absolutely feels like a coordinated effort to cover it up and discredit me because I'm the one who noticed their fingerprints on the data. Whenever I point out the clear patterns of manipulation in the spreadsheets and emails, they just smile, dismiss it as being \"within tolerance,\" and treat me like I'm imagining things. It is deeply frustrating that they are trying to make me look crazy just because I'm the one who uncovered what they did.\n\nDoctor: Besides the low energy and loss of interest, have you noticed any decline in your personal hygiene, your ability to speak fluidly, or a tendency to show less facial expression or emotion in your daily interactions?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed a decline in my hygiene, but I definitely feel emotionally flat and show very little expression these days. It has also become much harder to engage in conversation; I talk very little now and usually only give my wife a few words when she asks me a direct question.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that your speech has become less productive or that you have less spontaneous movement and facial expression compared to how you were before these two years began?"

While I do spend a lot of time just sitting around with very little energy or interest, I'm not sure if I'm physically slowed down in the way you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that your conviction about the data manipulation is the primary cause of your low mood, or did you feel this sense of being flat and disengaged even before you became concerned about the situation at work?\n\nPatient: The flat, disengaged feeling actually started much earlier, sliding gradually over the last two years as I stopped hiking and star-gazing. My certainty about the data being tampered with is much more recent, only developing over the last couple of months. So the lack of energy and withdrawal definitely came first.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced similar episodes of feeling this flat, low-energy, and disengaged in the past, or is this the first time you have felt this way for such a long period?\n\nPatient: No, I have never felt like this before, and this is the first time in my life I've experienced this kind of flat state. Before this slide started about two and a half years ago with a high-stakes work contract and software migration, I was very active and always had energy for hiking and star-gazing. This prolonged feeling of being completely disconnected and drained is entirely new to me.\n\nDoctor: Has there been any other time in your life, perhaps years ago, where you experienced a distinct period of a few weeks or more where you felt similarly low, flat, or lacked interest in your hobbies?\n\nPatient: No, I don't recall ever feeling this way or losing interest in my hobbies at any other time in my life prior to this. Before this situation started a couple of years ago, I was always consistently active and engaged with my interests. This is completely unprecedented for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that people were trying to harm you, spy on you, or that you were being targeted by others in any other situations outside of this specific work data issue?\n\nPatient: No, I've never felt spied on, targeted, or harmed in any other part of my life. My concern is entirely about the water-use data at work being secretly manipulated, and it's deeply frustrating that everyone just dismisses the clear proof I've found.\n\nDoctor: Besides the data manipulation at work, have you ever believed that you have special abilities, a secret mission, or that common events are specifically designed to send you a personal message?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, and I'm a bit confused why you'd ask that—I don't have any \"special powers\" or secret missions. I'm not crazy; my concern is strictly about the concrete, logical data manipulation I have mathematically proven in the spreadsheets at work. It's a real, objective issue with the numbers, not some kind of fantasy.\n\nDoctor: Since this is the first time you have experienced these symptoms, have you felt this way continuously for at least two years, or has there been any period during these last two years where you felt like your normal, energetic self?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had any breaks or times where I felt like my old self; it has been a continuous, steady slide for a little over two years now. My focus is actually intensely locked onto those data patterns, and I don't feel bad about myself; I am just extremely drained and have lost interest in everything else.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite, sleep patterns, or feelings of guilt or worthlessness during these past two years, or has it strictly been a loss of energy and interest?\n\nPatient: It has strictly been a loss of energy, interest, and emotion, as I don't feel guilty or worthless at all. My sleep is sometimes delayed because I stay up late analyzing the spreadsheets, but my appetite is normal and I don't feel bad about myself. I simply have no drive or energy to do or feel anything outside of this work issue.\n\nDoctor: Regarding the data manipulation you've discovered, do you believe that you are the only one who has the intelligence or special insight to uncover this, or do you feel you are being targeted by these people because of who you are?\n\nPatient: I'm not sure about being targeted or having unique intelligence, but as a professional, the mathematical patterns in the data are simply clear to me. Subtypes: Self-Induced Vomiting, Misuse of Medications\n[S049] Eating_Large_Amount: Eating, in a discrete period of time, an amount of food that is definitely larger than what most individuals would eat in a similar period of time under similar circumstances.\n[S050] Loss_of_Control: A sense of lack of control over eating during the episode (e.g., a feeling that one cannot stop eating or control what or how much one is eating).\n[S051] Rapid_Eating: Eating much more rapidly than normal.\n[S052] Eating_Until_Uncomfortably_Full: Eating until feeling uncomfortably full.\n[S053] Eating_Without_Hunger: Eating large amounts of food when not feeling physically hungry.\n[S054] Eating_Alone_Due_to_Embarrassment: Eating alone because of feeling embarrassed by how much one is eating.\n[S055] Negative_Affect_After_Binge: Feeling disgusted with oneself, depressed, or very guilty afterward.\n[S084] Low_Weight_Severity_Non_Recognition: Persistent lack of recognition of the seriousness of the current low body weight.  Subtypes: Denial of Medical Risk, Disregard of Symptoms, Dismissal of Concerns\n[S056] Excessive_Anxiety_Worry: Excessive anxiety and worry (expectant apprehension) occurring persistently about a number of events or activities.  Subtypes: Chronic Worry, Generalized Worry, Catastrophizing, Intolerance of Uncertainty\n[S057] Uncontrollable_Worry: The subjective experience of finding it difficult to stop or control the worry.  Subtypes: Failure to Control, Intrusive Worry, Chaining of Worry\n[S058] Restlessness_Tension: Restlessness, or feeling keyed up or on edge. Subtypes: Interference by Worry, Inability to Sustain Attention, Mind Going Blank\n[S060] Irritability: A state of reacting sensitively or sharply to minor stimuli.  Subtypes: Low Frustration Tolerance, Snappiness, Exhausted Patience\n[S061] Muscle_Tension: A state where muscles are continuously tense, stiff, and accompanied by discomfort. Subtypes: Animal Type, Natural Environment Type, Blood-Injection-Injury Type, Situational Type, Other Type\n[S063] Immediate_Fear/Anxiety_Response: The phobic object or situation almost always provokes immediate fear or anxiety. Subtypes: Contamination Obsessions, Pathological Doubt, Symmetry and Exactness Obsessions, Forbidden or Taboo Thoughts, Somatic Obsessions\n[S066] Compulsions: Repetitive behaviors or mental acts performed in response to an obsession or to prevent anxiety/distress; clearly excessive or not realistically connected.  Subtypes: Cleaning or Washing Compulsions, Checking Compulsions, Ordering and Arranging Compulsions, Mental Acts, Repeating Rituals\n[S067] Preoccupation_with_Appearance: Preoccupation with one or more perceived defects or flaws in physical appearance that are not observable or appear slight to others.  Subtypes: Skin Defects, Facial Features, Asymmetry, Muscle Dysmorphia, Specific Body Parts\n[S068] Appearance_Related_Repetitive_Behaviors: Repetitive behaviors or mental acts performed in response to appearance concerns.  Subtypes: Mirror Checking or Avoidance, Excessive Camouflaging, Skin Picking, Reassurance Seeking, Comparing, Seeking Cosmetic Procedures\n[S069] Intrusive_Symptoms: Recurrent, involuntary, and intrusive distressing memories of the traumatic event.  Subtypes: Intrusive Memories, Distressing Dreams, Dissociative Reactions (Flashbacks), Psychological Distress at Cues, Physiological Reactions to Cues\n[S070] Trauma_Avoidance: Persistent avoidance of stimuli associated with the traumatic event.  Subtypes: Internal Avoidance, External Avoidance\n[S071] Trauma_Related_Amnesia: Inability to remember an important aspect of the traumatic event (typically due to dissociative amnesia and not to other factors like head injury or alcohol).  Subtypes: Fragmented Memory, Generalized Amnesia\n[S072] Negative_Beliefs: Persistent and exaggerated negative beliefs or expectations about oneself, others, or the world.  Subtypes: Negative Self-Beliefs, Negative Beliefs about Others, Negative Worldview\n[S073] Distorted_Cognitions_With_Blame: Persistent, distorted cognitions about the cause or consequences of the traumatic event that lead the individual to blame himself/herself or others.  Subtypes: Self-Blame, Blaming Others\n[S074] Persistent_Negative_Emotional_State: Persistent negative emotional state (e.g., fear, horror, anger, guilt, or shame).  Subtypes: Persistent Fear, Persistent Horror, Persistent Anger, Persistent Guilt, Persistent Shame\n[S075] Social_Detachment: Feelings of detachment or estrangement from others.  Subtypes: Emotional Detachment, Social Isolation, Difficulty with Intimacy, Loss of Belonging\n[S076] Inability_Positive_Emotions: Persistent inability to experience positive emotions (e.g., inability to experience happiness, satisfaction, or loving feelings).  Subtypes: Anhedonia for Happiness, Lack of Satisfaction, Inability to Feel Love, Emotional Numbness\n[S077] Irritability_Aggression: Irritable behavior and angry outbursts (with little or no provocation) typically expressed as verbal or physical aggression.  Subtypes: Impulsive Anger, Verbal Aggression, Physical Aggression, Chronic Irritability\n[S078] Reckless_Self_Destructive_Behavior: Reckless or self-destructive behavior pattern that endangers one's safety or health.  Subtypes: Risky Behaviors, Substance Abuse, Indisicriminate Sexual Behavior, Self-Harm\n[S079] Hypervigilance: Persistent state of being alert and tense about the surrounding environment.  Subtypes: Scanning for Danger, Safety Checking, Sustained Alertness\n[S080] Exaggerated_Startle_Response: Exaggerated startle response to minor stimuli.  Subtypes: Auditory Startle, Tactile Startle, Visual Startle, Slow Recovery\n[S081] Stress_Related_Maladaptive_Response: Emotional or behavioral symptoms appearing in response to an identifiable stressor.  Subtypes: With Depressed Mood, With Anxiety, With Mixed Anxiety and Depressed Mood, With Disturbance of Conduct, With Mixed Disturbance of Emotions and Conduct, Unspecified\n[S082] Depersonalization_Derealization: Altered sense of the reality of one's self or surroundings after a traumatic event. Subtypes: Self-Induced Vomiting, Misuse of Medications\n[S049] Eating_Large_Amount: Eating, in a discrete period of time, an amount of food that is definitely larger than what most individuals would eat in a similar period of time under similar circumstances.\n[S050] Loss_of_Control: A sense of lack of control over eating during the episode (e.g., a feeling that one cannot stop eating or control what or how much one is eating).\n[S051] Rapid_Eating: Eating much more rapidly than normal.\n[S052] Eating_Until_Uncomfortably_Full: Eating until feeling uncomfortably full.\n[S053] Eating_Without_Hunger: Eating large amounts of food when not feeling physically hungry.\n[S054] Eating_Alone_Due_to_Embarrassment: Eating alone because of feeling embarrassed by how much one is eating.\n[S055] Negative_Affect_After_Binge: Feeling disgusted with oneself, depressed, or very guilty afterward.\n[S084] Low_Weight_Severity_Non_Recognition: Persistent lack of recognition of the seriousness of the current low body weight.  Subtypes: Denial of Medical Risk, Disregard of Symptoms, Dismissal of Concerns\n[S056] Excessive_Anxiety_Worry: Excessive anxiety and worry (expectant apprehension) occurring persistently about a number of events or activities. Subtypes: Interference by Worry, Inability to Sustain Attention, Mind Going Blank\n[S060] Irritability: A state of reacting sensitively or sharply to minor stimuli.  Subtypes: Low Frustration Tolerance, Snappiness, Exhausted Patience\n[S061] Muscle_Tension: A state where muscles are continuously tense, stiff, and accompanied by discomfort. Subtypes: Contamination Obsessions, Pathological Doubt, Symmetry and Exactness Obsessions, Forbidden or Taboo Thoughts, Somatic Obsessions\n[S066] Compulsions: Repetitive behaviors or mental acts performed in response to an obsession or to prevent anxiety/distress; clearly excessive or not realistically connected.  Subtypes: Cleaning or Washing Compulsions, Checking Compulsions, Ordering and Arranging Compulsions, Mental Acts, Repeating Rituals\n[S067] Preoccupation_with_Appearance: Preoccupation with one or more perceived defects or flaws in physical appearance that are not observable or appear slight to others.  Subtypes: Skin Defects, Facial Features, Asymmetry, Muscle Dysmorphia, Specific Body Parts\n[S068] Appearance_Related_Repetitive_Behaviors: Repetitive behaviors or mental acts performed in response to appearance concerns.  Subtypes: Mirror Checking or Avoidance, Excessive Camouflaging, Skin Picking, Reassurance Seeking, Comparing, Seeking Cosmetic Procedures\n[S069] Intrusive_Symptoms: Recurrent, involuntary, and intrusive distressing memories of the traumatic event.  Subtypes: Intrusive Memories, Distressing Dreams, Dissociative Reactions (Flashbacks), Psychological Distress at Cues, Physiological Reactions to Cues\n[S070] Trauma_Avoidance: Persistent avoidance of stimuli associated with the traumatic event.  Subtypes: Internal Avoidance, External Avoidance\n[S071] Trauma_Related_Amnesia: Inability to remember an important aspect of the traumatic event (typically due to dissociative amnesia and not to other factors like head injury or alcohol).  Subtypes: Fragmented Memory, Generalized Amnesia\n[S072] Negative_Beliefs: Persistent and exaggerated negative beliefs or expectations about oneself, others, or the world.  Subtypes: Negative Self-Beliefs, Negative Beliefs about Others, Negative Worldview\n[S073] Distorted_Cognitions_With_Blame: Persistent, distorted cognitions about the cause or consequences of the traumatic event that lead the individual to blame himself/herself or others.  Subtypes: Self-Blame, Blaming Others\n[S074] Persistent_Negative_Emotional_State: Persistent negative emotional state (e.g., fear, horror, anger, guilt, or shame).  Subtypes: Persistent Fear, Persistent Horror, Persistent Anger, Persistent Guilt, Persistent Shame\n[S075] Social_Detachment: Feelings of detachment or estrangement from others.  Subtypes: Emotional Detachment, Social Isolation, Difficulty with Intimacy, Loss of Belonging\n[S076] Inability_Positive_Emotions: Persistent inability to experience positive emotions (e.g., inability to experience happiness, satisfaction, or loving feelings).  Subtypes: Anhedonia for Happiness, Lack of Satisfaction, Inability to Feel Love, Emotional Numbness\n[S077] Irritability_Aggression: Irritable behavior and angry outbursts (with little or no provocation) typically expressed as verbal or physical aggression.  Subtypes: Impulsive Anger, Verbal Aggression, Physical Aggression, Chronic Irritability\n[S078] Reckless_Self_Destructive_Behavior: Reckless or self-destructive behavior pattern that endangers one's safety or health.  Subtypes: Risky Behaviors, Substance Abuse, Indisicriminate Sexual Behavior, Self-Harm\n[S079] Hypervigilance: Persistent state of being alert and tense about the surrounding environment.  Subtypes: Scanning for Danger, Safety Checking, Sustained Alertness\n[S080] Exaggerated_Startle_Response: Exaggerated startle response to minor stimuli.  Subtypes: Auditory Startle, Tactile Startle, Visual Startle, Slow Recovery\n[S081] Stress_Related_Maladaptive_Response: Emotional or behavioral symptoms appearing in response to an identifiable stressor.  Subtypes: With Depressed Mood, With Anxiety, With Mixed Anxiety and Depressed Mood, With Disturbance of Conduct, With Mixed Disturbance of Emotions and Conduct, Unspecified\n[S082] Depersonalization_Derealization: Altered sense of the reality of one's self or surroundings after a traumatic event.
